Given everything we've accomplished this session, here's where I see the highest-value next moves:

**Immediate (this session):**
1. **Commit the work.** We have ~8 new files, 3 modified files, and ~52 new tests across 5 workstreams — all uncommitted. A clean commit (or batched commits by workstream) would lock in the gains.

**Next session:**
2. **Wire SkillHealthTracker into real compound cycles.** The tracker exists but only fires when `CompoundExecutor.execute_task()` runs. Running a few real compound cycles would populate `data/skill_health.json` and give you the first real signal on which of your 124 skills are active vs. dead.

3. **Seed the vault decay system.** The decay scoring works but `last_accessed` and `access_count` are empty on all neurons. A one-time bulk annotation pass (set `last_accessed` to file modification date, `access_count` to backlink count) would bootstrap the system so search results immediately benefit from the new ranking.

**Medium-term:**
4. **CLAUDE.md is now 339 lines but could go further.** The "Coding Standards" and "Operational Patterns" sections overlap with your global rules (`coding-standards.md`, `python-rules.md`, `tdd-enforcement.md`). Deduplicating those would get CLAUDE.md under 250 lines.

5. **Skill pruning.** Once the health tracker has a week of data, run `get_stale_skills(days=30)` to identify which of the 124 PRIME skills have never been invoked. Likely 60-80% are dormant.

     1→## Coding Standards
     2→
     3→### Priority Order
     4→
     5→**Correctness > Maintainability > Performance > Brevity**
     6→
     7→### Core Principles
     8→
     9→- **DRY:** Extract duplicated logic into reusable functions immediately.
    10→- **YAGNI:** Build only what's explicitly required. No hypothetical abstractions.
    11→- **Single Responsibility:** Each function does one thing. If you need "and" to describe it, split it.
    12→
    13→### Naming
    14→
    15→Descriptive, intent-revealing names: `calculate_discount`, `validate_email`, `fetch_active_users`. Avoid: `process`, `handle`, `data`, `temp`, `x`.
    16→
    17→### Code Organization
    18→
    19→- **Imports:** stdlib → third-party → local. Remove unused immediately.
    20→- **Dead code:** Delete unused functions, commented-out blocks. Use VCS to recover.
    21→- **File size:** Production files under 300 lines. Above 500 = hard limit, refactor immediately.
    22→
    23→### Before Modifying Code
    24→
    25→**Dependency check:** `Grep`/LSP `findReferences` to find all callers. Plan updates for affected call sites. Catches breaking changes before you make them.
    26→
    27→### Self-Correction
    28→
    29→Fix obvious mistakes (syntax errors, typos, missing imports, off-by-one) immediately without asking. Reserve user communication for decisions, not trivial fix reports.
    30→
    31→### Quality Checks
    32→
    33→Check diagnostics before and after changes. Fix all errors. Let formatters handle style. Use `rg` over grep.
    34→
    35→### Versioning
    36→
    37→Semver: `MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH`. Breaking = major, features = minor, fixes = patch. Version in `pyproject.toml`.
    38→
```

     1→# CLAUDE.md
     2→
     3→This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.
     4→
     5→# Cohezion - Compound AI Orchestration
     6→
     7→COHEZION: 12D agentic universe with FLUME VAE, compound engineering, multi-agent swarm, and autonomous skill refinement. **Governed by Constitution (`.agent/CONSTITUTION.md`) and Charter (`.agent/COHEZION_CHARTER.md`).**
     8→
     9→## Token-Efficient Essentials
    10→
    11→### ⚡ Core Commands
    12→```bash
    13→uv run pytest tests/ -q              # Full test suite (3,200+ tests, ~90s)
    14→uv run pytest tests/compound/ -v     # Run module tests
    15→uv run pytest tests/test_*.py::name  # Single test
    16→make format && make lint && make all # Check → fix → verify
    17→```
    18→
    19→### ⚡ Security Standards (MANDATORY)
    20→```bash
    21→# NEVER print, echo, or display any passwords or secrets
    22→# NEVER store SUDO_PASSWORD in .env for scripts - use passwordless sudo
    23→# NEVER write secrets to temp files that persist
    24→
    25→# ALWAYS use 'uv' for package management - NEVER bare 'pip' or 'pip install'
    26→uv pip install package              # Install package
    27→uv venv && source .venv/bin/activate && uv pip install -e .  # New project setup
    28→
    29→# For sudo: configure passwordless sudo for automation OR run interactively
    30→# Do NOT attempt to parse .env for SUDO_PASSWORD - this is a security risk
    31→```
    32→
    33→### ⚡ Critical Principles (Sessions 40-55)
    34→1. **Implement ONE feature, validate manually, write 5 tests** (NOT 600 pre-implementation tests)
    35→2. **Use proven templates** (e.g., cloud-vault-mcp: 40+ tools) over greenfield exploration
    36→3. **Mock live services at source**: `@patch("cohezion.swarm.compound_client.get_compound_client")`
    37→4. **Report honest metrics** (98.8% beats inflated 100% for decision-making)
    38→5. **Never write infrastructure for products that don't exist**
    39→
    40→### ⚡ Vault-First Knowledge Management (NEW: Session 56)
    41→
    42→**CRITICAL**: All session learnings MUST be logged to vault, not MEMORY.md directly.
    43→
    44→**MEMORY.md = Compiled Cache** (auto-generated weekly):
    45→- 95 lines (vs 1177 lines old version)
    46→- Recent decisions (last 7 days)
    47→- Most-used patterns (top 10)
    48→- Quick reference only
    49→
    50→**Vault = Single Source of Truth**:
    51→- `~/vaults/cohezion-vault/` (150+ decisions, patterns, experiments)
    52→- Searchable via `vault_find_relevant_context(query)`
    53→- Survives across sessions, compounds knowledge
    54→
    55→**How to Log Learnings**:
    56→```python
    57→# Log architectural decisions
    58→vault_log_decision(
    59→    project="cohezion",
    60→    title="Short decision title",
    61→    context="What led to this decision",
    62→    decision="What was decided",
    63→    rationale="Why this option was chosen"
    64→)
    65→
    66→# Log experiments (what was tried & learned)
    67→vault_log_experiment(
    68→    project="cohezion",
    69→    hypothesis="What you expected",
    70→    method="What you did",
    71→    result="What happened",
    72→    learnings="Key takeaways"
    73→)
    74→
    75→# Extract reusable patterns
    76→vault_extract_pattern(
    77→    source_path="path/to/source",
    78→    pattern_name="Pattern Name",
    79→    description="When to use this pattern",
    80→    code_example="```python\n# example\n```",
    81→    domain="testing|mcp|compound-engineering|etc"
    82→)
    83→```
    84→
    85→**Regenerate MEMORY.md**:
    86→```bash
    87→# Run weekly or after major learnings
    88→uv run python scripts/compile_memory_from_vault.py
    89→```
    90→
    91→**Token Savings**: 10K+ tokens/session (load only relevant context via search vs loading all 1177 lines)
    92→
    93→### ⚡ Architecture at a Glance
    94→| Layer | Components | Entry |
    95→|-------|-----------|-------|
    96→| **Compound** | Executor, SkillRefiner, RetrospectionEngine, JourneyTracker | `CompoundExecutor` |
    97→| **Swarm** | TeamOrchestrator, ExecutionOrchestrator, DynamicModelRouter | `TeamExecutor` |
    98→| **Cache** | SemanticCache (L1 hash + L2 cosine + L3 vault, 95%+ hit rate) | `SemanticCache` |
    99→| **Cost Opt** | CostAwareRouter (27.3% savings), BudgetEnforcer, ModelQualityClassifier | `CostAwareRouter` |
   100→| **Persistence** | SessionPersistence (vault + JSONL), MetricsCollector, DegradationDetector | `SessionManager` |
   101→| **Knowledge** | Vault-First (decisions/patterns/experiments), auto-compiled MEMORY.md | `vault_find_relevant_context` |
   102→
   103→### ⚡ Quick Reference
   104→- **Language**: Python 3.13+ | **Package Manager**: `uv` (never bare python)
   105→- **DB**: SurrealDB (ws://localhost:8000) | **API**: FastAPI :8080
   106→- **Tests**: 3,214 passing / 4 failing (99.9%) | **Coverage**: html report in `htmlcov/`
   107→- **CI**: `make lint-check && uv run pytest` before commit
   108→- **Entry point**: `cohezion = "cohezion.__main__:main"`
   109→- **Vault**: `~/vaults/cohezion-vault/` — Query via `vault_find_relevant_context(query)`
   110→
   111→## The Compound Engineering Loop (Production-Ready)
   112→
   113→```
   114→PRIME Skill (markdown)
   115→  ↓
   116→InstructionExpander (parse → tasks)
   117→  ↓
   118→PlanExecutor (tactical plan)
   119→  ↓
   120→ExecutionOrchestrator (execute with 11-step pipeline)
   121→  ├─ RequestAlignmentAnalyzer (coherence check)
   122→  ├─ GlobalMetricsAggregator (record instance metrics)
   123→  ├─ DegradationDetector (thermal, quality thresholds)
   124→  └─ JourneyTracker (12D universe position)
   125→  ↓
   126→RetrospectionEngine (extract learnings, flag anomalies)
   127→  ↓
   128→SkillRefiner (update skill definition)
   129→  ↓
   130→SkillConsensusVoter (multi-agent validation)
   131→  ↓
   132→Updated Skill (loop again)
   133→```
   134→**CLI**: `uv run python scripts/drivers/compound_cycle.py` (dry-run) or via `/compound` API endpoint.
   135→
   136→## Key Directories (Find Anything Fast)
   137→
   138→| Path | Purpose | Key Files |
   139→|------|---------|-----------|
   140→| `src/cohezion/compound/` | Executor, SkillRefiner, RetrospectionEngine, JourneyTracker | `executor.py` (11-step) |
   141→| `src/cohezion/swarm/` | Team orchestration, cost routing, model quality | `team_executor.py`, `cost_aware_router.py` |
   142→| `src/cohezion/cache/` | L1/L2/L3 semantic cache (95%+ hit rate) | `semantic_cache.py` |
   143→| `src/cohezion/skills/` | 124 PRIME skill definitions (*.md + *.py) | `skill_registry.json` |
   144→| `src/cohezion/persistence/` | SurrealDB, checkpoints, session recovery | `surreal_client.py` |
   145→| `src/cohezion/api/` | FastAPI backend (72 endpoints) | `__init__.py` (FastMCP patterns) |
   146→| `src/cohezion/flume/` | FLUME VAE (256D latent space) | `flume_vae.py` |
   147→| `tests/conftest.py` | **CRITICAL**: Singleton reset for FLUME VAE, RL policy, loggers | **Read this first** |
   148→
   149→## Coding Standards (Compound-Ready)
   150→
   151→- **Type hints**: Mandatory (mypy --strict). Enables alignment analysis at compile-time
   152→- **Docstrings**: NumPy-style. Document "why" intent, state assumptions for request alignment
   153→- **Async**: All I/O must be `async/await` with timeouts. No blocking calls in executors
   154→- **Error handling**: Specific exceptions + circuit breakers (`cohezion.reliability.get_circuit()`)
   155→- **Validation**: Pydantic at boundaries (input/output). Fail fast with assertions
   156→- **KISS**: Simple logic beats multi-agent swarms. Measure first, optimize later
   157→- **Every `src/` dir**: MUST have `__init__.py`. Enables vault skill discovery
   158→- **Observability**: Log state transitions (input → processing → output). Track coherence. Measure alignment
   159→
   160→### Journey Tracking Checklist (Compound Loop)
   161→When implementing features, add:
   162→1. **Input logging**: `journey_tracker.record_request(alignment_score)` at entry
   163→2. **State changes**: Record before/after for rollback capability
   164→3. **Metrics**: Call `metrics_collector.record_execution()` at completion
   165→4. **Coherence**: Check `degradation_detector.check_coherence()` before proceeding
   166→5. **Reflection**: Populate RetrospectionEngine output for skill refinement
   167→
   168→### Alignment Assessment (Before Execution)
   169→```python
   170→from cohezion.compound.request_alignment_analyzer import RequestAlignmentAnalyzer
   171→
   172→analyzer = RequestAlignmentAnalyzer()
   173→alignment = analyzer.analyze(request_state, available_skills, agent_context)
   174→if alignment.coherence < 0.5:  # HIHO threshold
   175→    logger.warning(f"Low coherence: {alignment.issues}")
   176→    # Escalate or fallback
   177→```
   178→
   179→## Token Budgets (Conservative Estimates)
   180→
   181→| Task | Tokens | Pattern |
   182→|------|--------|---------|
   183→| Implement 1 feature | 500-1,500 | Copy template → code → manual test → 5 tests |
   184→| Research + implement | 2,000-3,000 | Quick survey → proof of concept → tests |
   185→| Full test suite run | 100 | `uv run pytest tests/ -q` (no code changes) |
   186→| Single test debug | 200-500 | Add print → run → check output → fix → verify |
   187→| Skill refinement loop | 1,500-2,500 | Analyze failures → update PRIME skill → retest |
   188→| **ANTI-PATTERN**: Research-first | 5,000-10,000 | 1,200 lines research + 600 tests + 0 implementation = wasted |
   189→| **ANTI-PATTERN**: Infrastructure play | 8,000+ | Dependency research, 4,400 placeholder tests, product doesn't exist |
   190→
   191→**Rule**: If feature doesn't exist yet, DON'T build infrastructure. Implement, validate, THEN test.
   192→
   193→## Operational Patterns
   194→
   195→### ⚡ Test Isolation (Critical)
   196→Tests fail only as full suite? **NOT a logic bug — singleton pollution**. Fix in `tests/conftest.py`:
   197→- FLUME VAE: `cohezion.api._vae_trainer = None` (checkpoint mismatch causes NaN)
   198→- RL Policy: `cohezion.api._rl_policy = None` (bad reward state persists)
   199→- Loggers: `logging.getLogger().handlers.clear()` (formatters corrupt across files)
   200→
   201→### ⚡ Mocking External Services
   202→```python
   203→# CORRECT: Mock at source
   204→@patch("cohezion.swarm.compound_client.get_compound_client")
   205→
   206→# WRONG: Mocking after import fails randomly
   207→with patch("cohezion.api.compound_client"):  # Import already happened
   208→```
   209→
   210→### ⚡ Measurement Integrity
   211→- **Report actual numbers**: 2,675/2,700 passing (98.1%) > 1,599 passing (inflated)
   212→- **Flag discrepancies**: If independent test shows different count, request re-verification immediately
   213→- **Verify claims**: "Complete" = files exist AND tests pass, not projected
   214→
   215→### ⚡ Verification Protocol
   216→Before declaring task complete:
   217→1. **Code**: Reads logically? Type hints present?
   218→2. **Tests**: `uv run pytest tests/module/ -v` passes? No warnings?
   219→3. **Metrics**: Honest count? Zero regressions?
   220→4. **Alignment**: Does code match what was requested?
   221→5. **Rollback**: Can prior version recover? (State transitions logged?)
   222→

    10→---
    11→
    12→## TDD and Testing Standards
    13→
    14→**Core Rule:** No production code without a failing test first. Unit tests for logic, integration tests for interactions, E2E tests for workflows. Minimum 80% coverage.
    15→
    16→### The Red-Green-Refactor Cycle
    17→
    18→Follow this exact sequence for every feature, function, or behavior change:
    19→
    20→1. **RED** — Write one minimal failing test. Name: `test_<function>_<scenario>_<expected_result>`
    21→2. **VERIFY RED** — Execute test, confirm it fails because the feature doesn't exist (not syntax errors)
    22→3. **GREEN** — Write the simplest code that makes the test pass. No extras.
    23→4. **VERIFY GREEN** — Execute test, confirm it passes. Run all tests to check regressions.
    24→5. **REFACTOR** — Improve code quality while keeping tests green.
    25→
    26→**If test passes immediately:** You're testing existing behavior. Rewrite the test.
    27→
    28→**Recovery:** If you already wrote production code before tests, write tests immediately (they'll pass — that's fine). Apply TDD properly for remaining work.
    29→
    30→### When TDD Applies
    31→
    32→**Always:** New functions/methods, API endpoints, business logic, bug fixes (reproduce first), behavior changes.
    33→
    34→**Skip:** Documentation-only, config updates, dependency versions, formatting-only.
    35→
    36→### Test Type Selection
    37→
    38→| Type | When | Requirements |
    39→|------|------|-------------|
    40→| **Unit** | Pure functions, business logic, data transformations, validation | < 1ms each, mock ALL external deps, `@pytest.mark.unit` |
    41→| **Integration** | DB queries, external APIs, file system, auth flows | Real test deps, setup/teardown fixtures, `@pytest.mark.integration` |
    42→| **E2E** | Complete user workflows, API chains, data pipelines | Full system, `playwright-cli` for UI |
    43→
    44→### Mandatory Mocking in Unit Tests
    45→
    46→**Mock ALL external calls. No exceptions.**
    47→
    48→| Call Type | Must Mock | Pattern |
    49→|-----------|-----------|---------|
    50→| HTTP/Network | `httpx`, `requests` | `@patch("module.httpx.Client")` |
    51→| Subprocess | `subprocess.run` | `@patch("module.subprocess.run")` |
    52→| File I/O | `open`, `Path.read_text` | `@patch("builtins.open")` or `tmp_path` |
    53→| Database | Any DB connection | Test fixtures |
    54→| External APIs | Any third-party | Mock the client |
    55→
    56→**Mock at module level** (where imported, not where defined):
    57→```python
    58→@patch("mymodule.httpx.Client")
    59→def test_fetch_data(mock_client):
    60→    mock_response = MagicMock(status_code=200)
    61→    mock_response.json.return_value = {"data": "test"}
    62→    mock_client.return_value.__enter__.return_value.get.return_value = mock_response
    63→    result = fetch_data()
    64→    assert result == {"data": "test"}
    65→```
    66→
    67→**Detection:** Test takes > 1s = likely unmocked I/O. "Connection refused" = unmocked network. Hangs = unmocked blocking call.
    68→
    69→### Coverage Requirements
    70→
    71→Run: `uv run pytest --cov=src --cov-report=term-missing --cov-fail-under=80`
    72→
    73→**Must cover:** All business logic, API endpoints, data transformations, validation rules, error handling, conditional branches.
    74→
    75→**Exclude:** `if __name__ == "__main__"`, generated code, config files, simple getters/setters.
    76→
    77→### Common Anti-Patterns
    78→
    79→- **Dependent tests** — Test2 assumes test1 ran. Each test must be independent.
    80→- **Testing implementation** — Test behavior (`obj.get_total()`) not internals (`obj._counter`).
    81→- **Test-only production methods** — Create test utilities instead.
    82→- **Incomplete mock data** — Mock data must match real API structure completely.
    83→- **Asserting on mock existence** — Test real behavior, not that mocks are present.
    84→- **Ignoring failing tests** — Fix or remove immediately.
    85→- **Real network calls in unit tests** — NEVER.
    86→
    87→### Completion Checklist
    88→
    89→- [ ] Every new function has at least one test
    90→- [ ] Watched each test fail before implementing (or recovery mode)
    91→- [ ] All tests pass (executed and verified)
    92→- [ ] Coverage >= 80% verified
    93→- [ ] No flaky or dependent tests
    94→- [ ] Unit tests mock all external dependencies
    95→- [ ] Actual program executed and verified
    96→
```

     1→## Verification Before Completion
     2→
     3→**Core Rule:** Tests passing ≠ Program working. Evidence before claims. Never claim success without fresh verification output.
     4→
     5→### Mandatory Execution
     6→
     7→Run the actual program after tests pass. Tests use mocks and fixtures — they don't prove the real program works.
     8→
     9→**Execute after:** tests pass, refactoring, modifying imports/dependencies, changing configuration, adding features, before marking any task complete.
    10→
    11→**If there's a runnable program, RUN IT.**
    12→
    13→### How to Execute by Type
    14→
    15→| Type | Run | Verify |
    16→|------|-----|--------|
    17→| Scripts/CLI | `python script.py --args` | Exit code, stdout/stderr, file changes |
    18→| API Services | `python -m uvicorn app:app` + `curl` | Response status, payload, DB changes |
    19→| Build Artifacts | `python -m build` then run the built artifact | Package works, not just source |
    20→| Frontend/Web | `playwright-cli open` + `snapshot` | UI renders, forms work, navigation functions |
    21→
    22→### Verification Workflow
    23→
    24→Before ANY claim of success:
    25→
    26→1. **Identify** — What command proves this claim?
    27→2. **Execute** — Run the FULL command (not partial, not cached)
    28→3. **Read** — Check exit code, count failures, read full output
    29→4. **Confirm** — Does output actually prove the claim?
    30→5. **Report** — State claim WITH evidence
    31→
    32→**If you haven't run the command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.**
    33→
    34→### What Requires Verification
    35→
    36→| Claim | Required Evidence | Insufficient |
    37→|-------|-------------------|--------------|
    38→| "Tests pass" | Fresh test run: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
    39→| "Build succeeds" | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing |
    40→| "Bug fixed" | Test reproducing bug passes | Code changed |
    41→| "Output is correct" | Compare against source data | Logs look reasonable |
    42→| "UI works" | playwright-cli snapshot shows correct state | "API returns 200" |
    43→
    44→### Output Correctness
    45→
    46→**Running without errors ≠ Correct output.** If code processes external data, fetch that data independently and compare.
    47→
    48→**Rule:** Numbers and content MUST match. If code says "processed 1 item" but source has "18 items" = BUG.
    49→
    50→### Fix ALL Errors — No Exceptions, No Asking
    51→
    52→When verification reveals errors, fix ALL of them silently. Never ask "Should I fix these?" or "Want me to address these findings?" — just fix them. The user trusts you to leave the codebase better than you found it.
    53→
    54→In /spec workflows: must_fix and should_fix issues are fixed automatically without asking.
    55→
    56→### Stop Signals
    57→
    58→Run verification immediately if you're about to:
    59→- Use uncertain language: "should", "probably", "seems to"
    60→- Express satisfaction: "Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!"
    61→- Commit, push, or create PR
    62→- Mark task complete or move to next task
    63→
    64→### Evidence Required
    65→
    66→Show concrete evidence, not assumptions:
    67→
    68→- **Required:** "Ran `python app.py` - output: [paste logs]"
    69→- **Insufficient:** "Tests pass so it should work"
    70→
    71→### When to Skip Execution
    72→
    73→Skip ONLY for: documentation-only changes, test-only changes, pure internal refactoring (no entry points affected).
    74→
    75→**Default action: Execute.**
    76→
    77→### When Execution Fails After Tests Pass
    78→
    79→This is a real bug. Fix immediately, run tests again, execute again, add test to catch this failure type.
    80→
    81→### Common Issues Caught by Execution
    82→
    83→Import errors, missing dependencies, configuration errors, build issues, path issues — all caught by execution, missed by mocked tests.
    84→

     5→
     6→## Python Development Standards
     7→
     8→**Standards:** Always use uv | pytest for tests | ruff for quality | Self-documenting code
     9→
    10→### Package Management — UV ONLY
    11→
    12→`uv pip install`, `uv run python`, `uv run pytest`. **Never bare `pip`.**
    13→
    14→### Testing & Quality — MINIMIZE OUTPUT
    15→
    16→```bash
    17→uv run pytest -q                                    # Quiet mode (ALWAYS)
    18→uv run pytest -q --cov=src --cov-fail-under=80     # Coverage
    19→ruff format . && ruff check . --fix                 # Format + lint
    20→basedpyright src                                    # Type check
    21→```
    22→
    23→**Never use `-v`/`-s` unless debugging a specific test.** Verbose output burns context.
    24→
    25→For many errors, fix incrementally — don't dump 100+ errors into context.
    26→
    27→### Code Style
    28→
    29→- **Type hints:** Required on public functions. Modern syntax: `list[int]`, `str | None`
    30→- **Docstrings:** One-line for most. Multi-line only for complex logic. Skip if name is self-explanatory.
    31→- **Imports:** stdlib → third-party → local. `ruff check . --fix` auto-sorts.
    32→- **No bare `except`.** Catch specific exceptions.
    33→- **Use `pathlib`** over `os.path`. Use context managers for resources.
    34→
    35→### Project Config
    36→
    37→Python 3.12+. Dependencies in `pyproject.toml`. Tests use `@pytest.mark.unit`/`@pytest.mark.integration`.
    38→
    39→### Verification
    40→
    41→`uv run pytest -q` → `ruff format .` → `ruff check .` → `basedpyright src` → coverage >= 80% → no file > 300 lines.
    42→

# CLAUDE.md Further Reduction: 339 → ~210 lines

## Context

CLAUDE.md was reduced from 700 to 339 lines by extracting 5 skills. However, it still contains content that duplicates global rules and residual content that should have been fully extracted. This pass removes those overlaps to get under 250 lines.

## Overlap Analysis

| CLAUDE.md Section | Lines | Duplicated By | Action |
|-------------------|-------|---------------|--------|
| Journey Tracking Checklist (160-166) | 7 | `cohezion-journey-tracking` skill | Remove — was supposed to be extracted but left behind |
| Alignment Assessment code (168-177) | 10 | `cohezion-journey-tracking` skill | Remove — same residual |
| Test Isolation (195-199) | 5 | `cohezion-debugging-scenarios` skill | Remove — already in skill |
| Mocking External Services (201-208) | 8 | `tdd-enforcement.md` global rule (lines 44-67) | Remove — exact same content |
| Verification Protocol (215-221) | 7 | `verification-before-completion.md` global rule (83 lines) | Remove — subset of comprehensive rule |
| Vault-First code examples (55-91) | 37 | None yet | Extract to new `cohezion-vault-workflow` skill |
| Skill Routing tables (275-326) | 52 | None yet | Extract to new `cohezion-skill-routing` skill |
| Coding Standards generic items (149-158) | ~5 | `coding-standards.md` + `python-rules.md` | Condense — keep only Cohezion-specific items |
| **Total removable** | **~131** | | **339 → ~208 lines** |

## Plan

### Step 1: Remove residual content (already in skills)

**Lines 160-177** (Journey Tracking Checklist + Alignment Assessment code): Replace with:
```
See skill: `cohezion-journey-tracking` for implementation checklist and alignment assessment API.
```

**Lines 195-199** (Test Isolation): Already covered by `cohezion-debugging-scenarios` skill. Replace the "Operational Patterns" section header + Test Isolation with:
```
### ⚡ Operational Patterns
See skill: `cohezion-debugging-scenarios` for test isolation, singleton resets, and flaky test fixes.
```

### Step 2: Remove content duplicated by global rules

**Lines 201-208** (Mocking External Services): Exact duplicate of `tdd-enforcement.md` lines 44-67. Remove entirely — the global rule fires automatically for all test files via `paths: ["**/test_*.py", ...]`.

**Lines 215-221** (Verification Protocol): Subset of `verification-before-completion.md` global rule. Remove entirely.

### Step 3: Extract Vault-First code examples to skill

**Lines 55-91** (37 lines of vault API examples + regenerate command): Create `.claude/skills/cohezion-vault-workflow.md`:
- Trigger: "vault log decision", "vault log experiment", "vault extract pattern", "vault workflow", "log learnings", "MEMORY.md"
- Content: vault_log_decision, vault_log_experiment, vault_extract_pattern examples + MEMORY.md regeneration

Replace in CLAUDE.md with:
```
See skill: `cohezion-vault-workflow` for vault API examples (log decisions, experiments, patterns) and MEMORY.md regeneration.
```

Keep the introductory lines 40-54 (Vault = Single Source of Truth, MEMORY.md = Compiled Cache) — those are project identity.

### Step 4: Extract Skill Routing to skill

**Lines 275-326** (52 lines of routing tables): Create `.claude/skills/cohezion-skill-routing.md`:
- Trigger: "which skill", "skill routing", "what skill to use", "bmad help", "which command", "route request"
- Content: Decision tree, keyword-to-skill table, overlap resolution table

Replace in CLAUDE.md with:
```
## Skill Routing
See skill: `cohezion-skill-routing` for the decision tree, keyword-to-skill routing table, and overlap resolution guide.
```

### Step 5: Condense Coding Standards to Cohezion-specific only

Current (lines 149-158) mixes generic standards (type hints, docstrings — covered by global rules) with Cohezion-specific items (async, circuit breakers, Pydantic, `__init__.py`, observability). Keep only what's unique:

```markdown
## Coding Standards (Cohezion-Specific)
- **Async**: All I/O must be `async/await` with timeouts. No blocking calls in executors
- **Error handling**: Specific exceptions + circuit breakers (`cohezion.reliability.get_circuit()`)
- **Validation**: Pydantic at boundaries (input/output). Fail fast with assertions
- **Every `src/` dir**: MUST have `__init__.py`. Enables vault skill discovery
- **Observability**: Log state transitions (input → processing → output). Track coherence
```

Remove: type hints (in `python-rules.md`), docstrings (in `python-rules.md`), KISS (in `coding-standards.md`).

### Step 6: Condense Measurement Integrity

Keep lines 210-213 (Measurement Integrity) — this is Cohezion-specific culture, not in any global rule.

## Files Modified

- `CLAUDE.md` — remove ~131 lines of duplicate/extractable content
- `.claude/skills/cohezion-vault-workflow.md` — new skill (vault API examples)
- `.claude/skills/cohezion-skill-routing.md` — new skill (routing tables)

## Verification

1. `wc -l CLAUDE.md` — should be ~208 lines (under 250 target)
2. Verify no content was lost — all extracted content lives in skills or global rules

  "content": "---\nname: cohezion-vault-workflow\ndescription: Vault-first knowledge management workflow for Cohezion. Covers vault_log_decision, vault_log_experiment, vault_extract_pattern API examples, MEMORY.md regeneration, and token savings strategy. Use when logging learnings to vault, extracting patterns, logging decisions or experiments, regenerating MEMORY.md, or when user mentions \"vault workflow\", \"log learnings\", \"log decision\", \"log experiment\", \"extract pattern\".\n---\n\n# Vault-First Knowledge Management\n\n**CRITICAL**: All session learnings MUST be logged to vault, not MEMORY.md directly.\n\n## How to Log Learnings\n\n```python\n# Log architectural decisions\nvault_log_decision(\n    project=\"cohezion\",\n    title=\"Short decision title\",\n    context=\"What led to this decision\",\n    decision=\"What was decided\",\n    rationale=\"Why this option was chosen\"\n)\n\n# Log experiments (what was tried & learned)\nvault_log_experiment(\n    project=\"cohezion\",\n    hypothesis=\"What you expected\",\n    method=\"What you did\",\n    result=\"What happened\",\n    learnings=\"Key takeaways\"\n)\n\n# Extract reusable patterns\nvault_extract_pattern(\n    source_path=\"path/to/source\",\n    pattern_name=\"Pattern Name\",\n    description=\"When to use this pattern\",\n    code_example=\"```python\\n# example\\n```\",\n    domain=\"testing|mcp|compound-engineering|etc\"\n)\n```\n\n## Regenerate MEMORY.md\n\n```bash\n# Run weekly or after major learnings\nuv run python scripts/compile_memory_from_vault.py\n```\n\n## Token Savings\n\n10K+ tokens/session by loading only relevant context via `vault_find_relevant_context(query)` instead of loading all 1177 lines of the old MEMORY.md.\n"
}

  "content": "---\nname: cohezion-skill-routing\ndescription: Skill routing decision tree and keyword-to-skill mapping for Cohezion's 7-layer skill ecosystem. Covers lifecycle phase routing, domain routing (BMM vs GDS), keyword tables, overlap resolution (spec vs quick-spec, BMM vs PR toolkit, retrospect vs BMM retrospective). Use when deciding which skill to use, routing a request, resolving skill ambiguity, or when user asks \"which skill\", \"what command\", \"help me route\".\n---\n\n# Skill Routing Quick Reference\n\n## Decision Tree (Priority Order)\n\nWhen a natural language request arrives, route using this priority:\n\n1. **Exact match** — Does it match a `/slash-command`? Execute directly\n2. **Lifecycle phase** — What phase of work?\n   - Research/Analysis: `bmad-bmm-technical-research`, `bmad-bmm-domain-research`, `bmad-bmm-market-research`\n   - Planning/Design: `bmad-bmm-create-prd`, `bmad-bmm-create-architecture`, `bmad-bmm-create-ux-design`\n   - Implementation: `bmad-bmm-dev-story`, `bmad-bmm-quick-dev`, or `/spec` (structured TDD)\n   - Testing: `bmad-tea-testarch-test-design`, `test-fix`\n   - Review: `bmad-bmm-code-review`, `pr-review-toolkit:review-pr`\n   - Maintenance: `retrospect`, `bmad-bmm-correct-course`, `deploy`, `heal`\n3. **Domain** — Game dev? Use `bmad-gds-*` variant. General product? Use `bmad-bmm-*`\n4. **Meta** — About BMAD itself? Use `bmad-bmb-*` (agent/workflow/module builder)\n5. **Creative** — Ideation/innovation? `bmad-brainstorming`, `bmad-cis-design-thinking`\n6. **Tool need** — External data? Library docs: `context7` | Papers: `cohezion-research` | GitHub: `github` MCP | Knowledge graph: `cohezion-surreal` | Multi-perspective: `cohezion-swarm`\n\n## Top Keyword-to-Skill Routing\n\n| Keywords | Primary Skill | Fallback |\n|----------|---------------|----------|\n| \"research\", \"investigate\" | `bmad-bmm-technical-research` | `bmad-bmm-domain-research` |\n| \"PRD\", \"requirements\" | `bmad-bmm-create-prd` | `bmad-bmm-edit-prd` |\n| \"architecture\", \"system design\" | `bmad-bmm-create-architecture` | — |\n| \"story\", \"epic\" | `bmad-bmm-create-story` | `bmad-bmm-create-epics-and-stories` |\n| \"sprint\", \"planning\" | `bmad-bmm-sprint-planning` | `bmad-bmm-sprint-status` |\n| \"implement\", \"build\", \"code this\" | `bmad-bmm-dev-story` | `bmad-bmm-quick-dev` |\n| \"test\", \"QA\" | `bmad-tea-testarch-test-design` | `test-fix` |\n| \"review\", \"code review\" | `bmad-bmm-code-review` | `pr-review-toolkit:review-pr` |\n| \"brainstorm\", \"ideate\" | `bmad-brainstorming` | `bmad-cis-design-thinking` |\n| \"game\", \"gameplay\" | Route to `bmad-gds-*` variant | — |\n| \"deploy\", \"ship\" | `deploy` | — |\n| \"fix tests\", \"failing tests\" | `test-fix` | — |\n| \"commit\", \"push\", \"PR\" | `commit-commands:commit` | `commit-commands:commit-push-pr` |\n| \"spec\", \"structured dev\" | `/spec` | — |\n| \"what now\", \"help\" | `bmad-help` | — |\n\n## Overlap Resolution (Key Ambiguities)\n\n| Ambiguity | Resolution |\n|-----------|------------|\n| BMM vs GDS variants (code-review, sprint, story) | Domain context: game project = GDS, otherwise = BMM |\n| `bmad-brainstorming` vs `superpowers:brainstorming` | `superpowers` is a meta-prompt enhancer; `bmad-brainstorming` is the interactive workflow |\n| `/spec` vs `bmad-bmm-quick-spec` | `/spec` = full TDD workflow; `quick-spec` = lightweight for small changes |\n| `bmad-bmm-code-review` vs `pr-review-toolkit:review-pr` | BMM = general code review; PR toolkit = PR-specific with GitHub integration |\n| `retrospect` vs `bmad-bmm-retrospective` | `retrospect` = dev-focused (flows into core files); BMM = product lifecycle |\n\n**When in doubt**: `bmad-help` — analyzes context and suggests the best next action.\n\n**Skill sources (7 layers)**: BMAD commands (~90), project commands (3), project skills (1), global commands (7), global rules (~15), plugin skills (~40), MCP tools (~80).\n"
}

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**CLAUDE.md journey this session: 700 → 339 → 211 lines (70% reduction)**

The key insight is that CLAUDE.md was doing three jobs simultaneously:
1. **Project identity** (what Cohezion is, architecture overview, hardware constraints) — must stay
2. **Reference documentation** (API examples, routing tables, debugging guides) — extracted to 7 skills
3. **Duplicated rules** (mocking patterns, verification protocol, coding standards) — eliminated since global rules already cover them

The result is a CLAUDE.md that's pure project identity — the minimum context needed to orient any session. Everything else loads on-demand through skills or inherits from global rules. That's ~3,500 fewer tokens loaded every single session.

Perfect! Now I have comprehensive information. Let me compile a complete reference for you:

## Claude Code Settings.json - Complete Configuration Reference (March 2026)

Based on official Claude Code documentation (v2.1.75+), here's the comprehensive settings reference for power users.

---

## 1. TOP-LEVEL SETTINGS.JSON KEYS (Complete List)

### Core Configuration

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `$schema` | string | - | JSON schema for autocomplete: `https://json.schemastore.org/claude-code-settings.json` |
| `model` | string | `"default"` | Override default model: `"opus"`, `"sonnet"`, `"haiku"`, `"opusplan"`, `"opus[1m]"`, `"sonnet[1m"]`, or version ID |
| `availableModels` | array | - | Restrict which models users can select via `/model` |
| `modelOverrides` | object | - | Map Anthropic model IDs to provider-specific IDs (Bedrock ARNs, Vertex names, Foundry deployments) |
| `effortLevel` | string | - | Persist adaptive reasoning effort: `"low"`, `"medium"`, `"high"`. Opus 4.6 & Sonnet 4.6 only |
| `alwaysThinkingEnabled` | boolean | false | Enable extended thinking by default for all sessions |

### Context & Token Management

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `autoCompactPrompt` | string | (model-specific) | Custom compaction strategy instructions when context reaches 95%+ |
| `cleanupPeriodDays` | number | 30 | Delete inactive sessions after N days; `0` disables persistence entirely |
| `showClearContextOnPlanAccept` | boolean | false | Show "clear context" option when accepting plans |

### Permissions & Security

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `permissions.allow` | array | - | Allow-list of tools/patterns (e.g., `["Bash(git *)"]`) |
| `permissions.deny` | array | - | Deny-list of tools/patterns (checked first, takes precedence) |
| `permissions.ask` | array | - | Require confirmation for these tools |
| `permissions.additionalDirectories` | array | - | Additional working directories Claude can access (beyond project root) |
| `permissions.defaultMode` | string | `"plan"` | Default permission mode: `"plan"`, `"auto"`, `"acceptEdits"`, `"dontAsk"`, `"bypassPermissions"` |
| `autoMode` | object | - | Customize auto mode classifier: `environment`, `allow`, `soft_deny` prose rules |
| `disableAutoMode` | string | - | Set to `"disable"` to remove auto mode from Shift+Tab cycle |
| `disableBypassPermissionsMode` | string | - | Set to `"disable"` to block `bypassPermissions` mode |

### Sandbox Configuration

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `sandbox.enabled` | boolean | false | Enable bash sandboxing (macOS, Linux, WSL2) |
| `sandbox.autoAllowBashIfSandboxed` | boolean | true | Auto-approve bash when sandboxed |
| `sandbox.excludedCommands` | array | - | Commands that run outside sandbox (e.g., `["docker", "git"]`) |
| `sandbox.allowUnsandboxedCommands` | boolean | true | Allow `dangerouslyDisableSandbox` escape hatch |
| `sandbox.filesystem.allowWrite` | array | - | Additional write paths (merged across scopes) |
| `sandbox.filesystem.denyWrite` | array | - | Paths blocked from writes |
| `sandbox.filesystem.denyRead` | array | - | Paths blocked from reads |
| `sandbox.filesystem.allowRead` | array | - | Re-allow reading within `denyRead` regions |
| `sandbox.filesystem.allowManagedReadPathsOnly` | boolean | false | (Managed only) Ignore user/project `allowRead` entries |
| `sandbox.network.allowedDomains` | array | - | Outbound domains allowed (supports `*.example.com` wildcards) |
| `sandbox.network.allowUnixSockets` | array | - | Unix socket paths (e.g., `["~/.ssh/agent-socket"]`) |
| `sandbox.network.allowAllUnixSockets` | boolean | false | Allow all Unix sockets |
| `sandbox.network.allowLocalBinding` | boolean | false | Allow binding to localhost (macOS only) |
| `sandbox.network.allowManagedDomainsOnly` | boolean | false | (Managed only) Ignore user/project `allowedDomains` |
| `sandbox.network.httpProxyPort` | number | - | HTTP proxy port (auto-run own proxy if unset) |
| `sandbox.network.socksProxyPort` | number | - | SOCKS5 proxy port |
| `sandbox.enableWeakerNestedSandbox` | boolean | false | Weaker isolation for unprivileged Docker (reduces security) |
| `sandbox.enableWeakerNetworkIsolation` | boolean | false | Allow system TLS trust on macOS (reduces security) |

### Hooks & Automation

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `hooks` | object | - | Lifecycle hooks: `SessionStart`, `InstructionsLoaded`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`, `PostToolUseFailure`, `PermissionRequest`, `SessionEnd`, etc. |
| `disableAllHooks` | boolean | false | Disable all hooks and custom status lines |
| `allowManagedHooksOnly` | boolean | false | (Managed only) Block user/project/plugin hooks |
| `allowedHttpHookUrls` | array | - | Allowlist for HTTP hook URLs (e.g., `["https://hooks.example.com/*"]`) |
| `httpHookAllowedEnvVars` | array | - | Allowlist of env vars HTTP hooks can use in headers |

### MCP & Plugins

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `enableAllProjectMcpServers` | boolean | false | Auto-approve all MCP servers in project `.mcp.json` |
| `enabledMcpjsonServers` | array | - | Specific MCP servers to approve (e.g., `["memory", "github"]`) |
| `disabledMcpjsonServers` | array | - | Specific MCP servers to reject (e.g., `["filesystem"]`) |
| `allowManagedMcpServersOnly` | boolean | false | (Managed only) Only apply allowlist from managed settings |
| `allowedMcpServers` | array | - | (Managed only) Allowlist of MCP servers by name |
| `deniedMcpServers` | array | - | (Managed only) Blocklist of MCP servers (takes precedence) |
| `enabledPlugins` | object | - | Control plugin state: `"plugin-name@marketplace": true/false` |
| `extraKnownMarketplaces` | object | - | Register additional plugin marketplaces |
| `strictKnownMarketplaces` | array | - | (Managed only) Allowlist of marketplace sources |
| `blockedMarketplaces` | array | - | (Managed only) Blocklist of marketplace sources |
| `pluginTrustMessage` | string | - | (Managed only) Custom message appended to plugin trust warning |

### Environment & Git

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `env` | object | - | Environment variables applied to every session (e.g., `{"FOO": "bar"}`) |
| `attribution.commit` | string | `"🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](...)\\n\\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 4.6..."` | Git commit attribution/trailers |
| `attribution.pr` | string | `"🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](...)"` | Pull request description attribution |
| `includeGitInstructions` | boolean | true | Include built-in git workflow instructions in system prompt |
| `respectGitignore` | boolean | true | Exclude `.gitignore` patterns from `@` file picker |

### Worktree Optimization

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `worktree.symlinkDirectories` | array | - | Symlink large dirs to avoid duplication: `["node_modules", ".cache"]` |
| `worktree.sparsePaths` | array | - | Sparse-checkout paths (faster in monorepos): `["packages/my-app", "shared/utils"]` |

### File & Output Configuration

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `fileSuggestion.type` | string | `"command"` | Type of file suggestion provider |
| `fileSuggestion.command` | string | - | Script for `@` file autocomplete |
| `statusLine.type` | string | - | Status line type: `"command"` or custom configuration |
| `statusLine.command` | string | - | Script that outputs custom status line |
| `outputStyle` | string | - | Output style: `"Explanatory"`, `"Learning"`, or custom |
| `plansDirectory` | string | `"~/.claude/plans"` | Where plan files are stored (relative to project root) |

### UI & Accessibility

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `prefersReducedMotion` | boolean | false | Reduce or disable UI animations |
| `spinnerTipsEnabled` | boolean | true | Show tips in spinner while Claude works |
| `spinnerTipsOverride.tips` | array | - | Custom tip strings for spinner |
| `spinnerTipsOverride.excludeDefault` | boolean | false | Hide built-in tips when custom tips set |
| `spinnerVerbs.mode` | string | `"append"` | `"append"` to add to defaults, `"replace"` to override |
| `spinnerVerbs.verbs` | array | - | Custom action verbs for spinner: `["Pondering", "Crafting"]` |
| `language` | string | - | Response language: `"japanese"`, `"spanish"`, `"french"`, etc. |

### Voice & Authentication

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `voiceEnabled` | boolean | false | Enable push-to-talk voice dictation |
| `forceLoginMethod` | string | - | Restrict login to `"claudeai"` or `"console"` |
| `forceLoginOrgUUID` | string | - | Auto-select organization UUID during login |

### Advanced Features

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `agent` | string | - | Run main thread as named subagent |
| `channelsEnabled` | boolean | - | (Managed only) Allow MCP channels for Team/Enterprise |
| `allowManagedPermissionRulesOnly` | boolean | false | (Managed only) Block user/project permission rules |
| `allowedHttpHookUrls` | array | - | Allowlist for HTTP hook targets |
| `fastModePerSessionOptIn` | boolean | false | Require explicit `/fast` each session (don't persist) |
| `feedbackSurveyRate` | number | - | Probability (0-1) of session quality survey |
| `autoUpdatesChannel` | string | `"latest"` | Update channel: `"latest"` or `"stable"` |
| `companyAnnouncements` | array | - | Messages to display at startup (rotated randomly) |

### Credential Management

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `apiKeyHelper` | string | - | Script to generate auth value (`X-Api-Key` & `Authorization` headers) |
| `otelHeadersHelper` | string | - | Script to generate dynamic OpenTelemetry headers |
| `awsAuthRefresh` | string | - | Script to refresh AWS credentials (e.g., `aws sso login --profile`) |
| `awsCredentialExport` | string | - | Script that outputs JSON with AWS credentials |

### Monitoring & Logging

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `autoMemoryDirectory` | string | - | Custom directory for auto memory storage |
| `apiKeyHelper` | string | - | Custom script to generate API keys |
| `otelHeadersHelper` | string | - | Script for dynamic OpenTelemetry headers |

## 2. GLOBAL CONFIG SETTINGS (`~/.claude.json`)

These settings are stored separately and NOT in settings.json:

| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|---------|
| `autoConnectIde` | boolean | false | Auto-connect to running IDE at startup |
| `autoInstallIdeExtension` | boolean | true | Auto-install Claude Code extension in VS Code |
| `editorMode` | string | `"normal"` | Input mode: `"normal"` or `"vim"` |
| `showTurnDuration` | boolean | true | Show "Cooked for 1m 6s" after responses |
| `terminalProgressBarEnabled` | boolean | true | Show progress bar (ConEmu, Ghostty 1.2.0+, iTerm2 3.6.6+) |

---

## 4. SETTINGS PRECEDENCE ORDER (Highest to Lowest)

1. **Managed settings** (server-managed, MDM/OS-level policies, or file) - cannot be overridden
2. **Command-line arguments** (e.g., `claude --model opus`)
3. **Local project settings** (`.claude/settings.local.json`)
4. **Shared project settings** (`.claude/settings.json`)
5. **User settings** (`~/.claude/settings.json`)

**Key rule**: Array settings (permissions, sandbox paths, MCP servers, etc.) **merge across scopes** rather than replace.

---

## 5. NEW IN 2025-2026 RELEASES

- **Effort levels** (`effortLevel`: `"low"`, `"medium"`, `"high"`, `"max"`): Adaptive reasoning for Opus 4.6 & Sonnet 4.6
- **Extended context** (`opus[1m]`, `sonnet[1m]`): 1M token window support
- **Agent teams** (`CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_AGENT_TEAMS=1`, `teammateMode`): Multi-agent coordination
- **1M context window** (`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_1M_CONTEXT` to opt-out)
- **Sandbox network isolation** (`sandbox.network.*`): Full isolation control
- **Dynamic MCP tool search** (`ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH=auto:15`): Context-aware tool discovery
- **Model overrides** (`modelOverrides`): Map Anthropic IDs to provider-specific values
- **Prompt caching per model** (`DISABLE_PROMPT_CACHING_*` granular control)

---

## 6. KEY PATTERNS FOR POWER USERS

### For 1M Context Sessions (Opus 4.6)
```json
{
  "model": "opus[1m]",
  "effortLevel": "high",
  "autoCompactPrompt": "Keep recent decisions/active plans. Archive old exploration."
}
```

### For Minimal Context Overhead
```json
{
  "env": {
    "CLAUDE_AUTOCOMPACT_PCT_OVERRIDE": "80",
    "CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW": "400000"
  }
}
```

### For Agent Teams (Experimental)
```json
{
  "env": {
    "CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_AGENT_TEAMS": "1"
  },
  "teammateMode": "tmux"
}
```

### For Enterprise / Managed Deployment
```json
{
  "allowManagedHooksOnly": true,
  "allowManagedPermissionRulesOnly": true,
  "allowManagedMcpServersOnly": true,
  "disableAutoMode": "disable"
}
```

---

## 7. FILES & LOCATIONS

| Scope | Location | Format |
|-------|----------|--------|
| **User** | `~/.claude/settings.json` | JSON |
| **User global** | `~/.claude.json` | JSON |
| **Project shared** | `.claude/settings.json` | JSON (checked in) |
| **Project local** | `.claude/settings.local.json` | JSON (gitignored) |
| **Managed (macOS)** | `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json` | JSON |
| **Managed (Linux/WSL)** | `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json` | JSON |
| **Managed (Windows)** | `C:\Program Files\ClaudeCode\managed-settings.json` | JSON |
| **Subagents** | `~/.claude/agents/` (user), `.claude/agents/` (project) | Markdown + YAML |
| **Skills** | `~/.claude/skills/` (user), `.claude/skills/` (project) | Markdown + YAML |
| **Memory** | `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` (user), `.claude/CLAUDE.md` or `CLAUDE.md` (project) | Markdown |

---

Your configuration in `/home/mike-anderson/.claude/settings.json` is already well-tuned for Opus 4.6 with aggressive context management (`DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY`, `autoCompactPrompt`), token optimization, and agent teams enabled. The key additions for 1M context work would be setting `effortLevel: "high"` and possibly `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW: "800000"` to trigger compaction at 95% of 800K rather than the full 1M window.

# Claude Code Configuration, Settings & Features Index

## Settings & Configuration Options

### Display & UI Settings
- `spinnerVerbs` (2.1.23) - Customizable spinner verbs
- `spinnerTipsOverride` (2.1.45) - Custom spinner tips with `tips` array and `excludeDefault` option
- `showClearContextOnPlanAccept` (2.1.81) - Show "clear context" option in plan mode (default: hidden in 2.1.81+)
- `/color` command (2.1.75) - Set prompt-bar color for session
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_TERMINAL_TITLE` (2.1.31) - Prevent terminal title from being set

### Performance & Optimization
- `cleanupPeriodDays` (2.1.83) - Tool result file cleanup period
- `sandbox.failIfUnavailable` (2.1.83) - Exit with error if sandbox enabled but unavailable
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONSTREAMING_FALLBACK` (2.1.83) - Disable non-streaming fallback when streaming fails
- `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBPROCESS_ENV_SCRUB=1` (2.1.83) - Strip credentials from subprocess environments
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_CRON` (2.1.72) - Stop scheduled cron jobs mid-session
- `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSIONEND_HOOKS_TIMEOUT_MS` (2.1.74) - Configure SessionEnd hooks timeout

### Voice Settings
- `voiceEnabled: true` (2.1.70) - Enable voice mode on startup
- `voice:pushToTalk` keybinding (2.1.71) - Make voice activation key rebindable (default: space)
- Language support for voice input (2.1.69) - 20 languages including Russian, Polish, Turkish, Dutch, Ukrainian, Greek, Czech, Danish, Swedish, Norwegian

### Model & Effort Settings
- `modelOverrides` (2.1.73) - Map model picker entries to custom provider model IDs (Bedrock ARNs, etc.)
- `ANTHROPIC_CUSTOM_MODEL_OPTION` (2.1.78) - Add custom entry to `/model` picker
- `ANTHROPIC_CUSTOM_MODEL_OPTION_NAME` (2.1.78) - Display name for custom model
- `ANTHROPIC_CUSTOM_MODEL_OPTION_DESCRIPTION` (2.1.78) - Description for custom model
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_1M_CONTEXT` (2.1.50) - Disable 1M context window support
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_BETAS` (2.1.81) - Suppress experimental betas beta header
- `/effort` command with low/medium/high levels (2.1.72)
- `effort` frontmatter for skills/agents (2.1.80, 2.1.76) - Override model effort level when invoked
- `alwaysThinkingEnabled: true` (2.1.47) - Enable thinking mode on Bedrock/Vertex

### Memory & Context Settings
- `autoMemoryDirectory` (2.1.74) - Configure custom directory for auto-memory storage
- `MEMORY.md` index truncation at 25KB and 200 lines (2.1.83)
- Last-modified timestamps added to memory files (2.1.75)

### Sandbox & Security Settings
- `sandbox.enabled: true` (2.1.78) - Enable sandboxing
- `sandbox.filesystem.allowWrite` (2.1.77) - Re-allow write access within `denyRead` regions
- `allowRead` sandbox filesystem setting (2.1.77) - Re-allow read access within `denyRead` regions
- `sandbox.excludedCommands` (2.1.72) - Exclude commands from sandboxing
- `dangerouslyDisableSandbox` (2.1.34) - Bypass sandboxing for specific commands
- `autoAllowBashIfSandboxed` (2.1.34) - Auto-allow Bash if sandboxed
- `sandbox.enableWeakerNetworkIsolation` (2.1.69) - Allow Go programs to verify TLS certificates (macOS only)
- `sandbox.enableWeakerNetworkIsolation` with `httpProxyPort` (2.1.69)
- `allowManagedDomainsOnly` (2.1.69) - Block non-allowed domains automatically
- `sandbox.filesystem.allowRead` (2.1.72) - Allow read access to specific paths
- `sandbox.filesystem.denyWrite` (2.1.72) - Deny write access to specific paths

### Plugin & Marketplace Settings
- `enabledPlugins` (2.1.80) - Plugins enabled via policy
- `extraKnownMarketplaces` (2.1.45) - Additional plugin marketplaces
- `strictKnownMarketplaces` with `hostPattern` and `pathPattern` (2.1.69) - Restrict marketplace sources
- `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_SEED_DIR` (2.1.79) - Multiple seed directories with platform path delimiter
- `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_CACHE_DIR` (2.1.72) - Custom plugin cache directory
- `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_GIT_TIMEOUT_MS` (2.1.51) - Plugin git timeout (default: 120s)
- `source: 'settings'` plugin source (2.1.80) - Declare plugins inline in settings.json
- `git-subdir` plugin source type (2.1.69) - Point to subdirectory within git repo

### MCP Server Settings
- `allowedMcpServers` (2.1.83) - Allowlist for MCP servers
- `deniedMcpServers` (2.1.83) - Denylist for MCP servers
- `oauth.authServerMetadataUrl` (2.1.69) - Custom OAuth metadata discovery URL for MCP servers
- MCP tool/resource deferred loading (2.1.80)
- MCP OAuth with CIMD/SEP-991 support (2.1.81)

### Permissions & Policy Settings
- `permissions.defaultMode` (2.1.80, 2.1.70) - Set default permission mode (acceptEdits, plan, etc.)
- `permissions.disableBypassPermissionsMode` (2.1.69) - Hide bypass permissions mode
- `deny: ["mcp__servername"]` permission rules (2.1.78) - Deny MCP server tools

### Git & Project Settings
- `includeGitInstructions` setting (2.1.69) - Remove git workflow instructions from system prompt
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_GIT_INSTRUCTIONS` (2.1.78) - Disable git instructions
- `worktree.sparsePaths` (2.1.76) - Sparse-checkout directories for `--worktree` in monorepos
- `--worktree` / `-w` flag (2.1.49) - Start in isolated git worktree
- `--continue` flag (2.1.72) - Resume from most recent point after compaction

### LSP & IDE Settings
- `startupTimeout` for LSP servers (2.1.50) - LSP server startup timeout configuration
- `/ide` command with auto-installation (2.1.73)
- IDE detection improvements (2.1.73)

### Remote & Bridge Settings
- `/remote-control` command with optional name argument (2.1.69)
- `disableDeepLinkRegistration` (2.1.83) - Prevent `claude-cli://` protocol handler registration
- Remote Control session titles (2.1.81, 2.1.76) - AI-generated from first message
- `--channels` permission relay (2.1.81, 2.1.80) - Allow MCP servers to forward approval prompts
- `--mcp-config` CLI flag (2.1.83) - MCP configuration file
- `allowManagedDomainsOnly` in sandbox (2.1.69) - Managed settings enforcement

### Custom Settings & Extensions
- `feedbackSurveyRate` (2.1.76) - Session quality survey sample rate
- `ANTHROPIC_BETAS` environment variable (2.1.78) - Beta features
- `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS` - SSL certificate errors from proxies
- `pluginTrustMessage` (2.1.69) - Organization-specific plugin trust warning
- `temperatureOverride` (2.1.31) - Override API temperature (streaming path)
- `macOptionClickForcesSelection` (2.1.77) - Force selection with option+click on macOS

### Agent Settings
- `agent_id` in hook events (2.1.69) - For subagents
- `agent_type` in hook events (2.1.69) - For subagents and `--agent`
- `model:` frontmatter for agents (2.1.74, 2.1.47) - Per-agent model override
- `effort` frontmatter for agents (2.1.78) - Per-agent effort level
- `maxTurns` frontmatter for agents (2.1.78) - Maximum turns for agent
- `disallowedTools` frontmatter for agents (2.1.78) - Tools agent cannot use
- `initialPrompt` in agent frontmatter (2.1.83) - Auto-submit first turn
- `background: true` for agents (2.1.49) - Always run as background task
- `isolation: "worktree"` for subagents (2.1.49, 2.1.50) - Run in isolated git worktree
- `memory` frontmatter for agents (2.1.45) - Persistent memory with user/project/local scope
- Agent teams feature (2.1.32) - Multi-agent collaboration (requires `CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_AGENT_TEAMS=1`)

### Skills & Commands Settings
- `effort` frontmatter support for skills (2.1.80) - Override effort when invoked
- Conditional `.claude/rules/*.md` with `paths:` frontmatter (2.1.69)
- `${CLAUDE_SKILL_DIR}` variable (2.1.69) - Reference skill's own directory
- Skill character budget scales with context window (2.1.32)
- Plugin-provided MCP server deduplication (2.1.71)

## Environment Variables

### API & Authentication
- `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` - Claude API key
- `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` - Custom API endpoint
- `ANTHROPIC_BETAS` (2.1.78) - Beta features header
- `ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH` (2.1.72) - Enable tool search
- `ENABLE_CLAUDEAI_MCP_SERVERS=false` (2.1.63) - Opt out of claude.ai MCP servers
- `CLAUDE_CODE_ACCOUNT_UUID` (2.1.51) - Account UUID for SDK
- `CLAUDE_CODE_USER_EMAIL` (2.1.51) - User email for SDK
- `CLAUDE_CODE_ORGANIZATION_UUID` (2.1.51) - Organization UUID for SDK

### Sandbox & Security
- `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBPROCESS_ENV_SCRUB=1` (2.1.83) - Strip credentials from subprocess environments
- `CLAUDE_BASH_NO_LOGIN` - Skip login shell flag in Bash tool

### Debugging & Diagnostics
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_CRON` (2.1.72) - Stop scheduled cron jobs
- `CLAUDE_CODE_SIMPLE` (2.1.50) - Minimal mode (strips skills, memory, agents, CLAUDE.md, MCP tools, attachments, hooks)
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_GIT_INSTRUCTIONS` (2.1.78) - Disable git instructions
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_TERMINAL_TITLE` (2.1.31) - Disable terminal title updates
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_1M_CONTEXT` (2.1.50) - Disable 1M context window
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_BETAS` (2.1.81, 2.1.25) - Suppress beta headers
- `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONSTREAMING_FALLBACK` (2.1.83) - Disable fallback when streaming fails
- `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` (2.1.69) - Max output tokens for compaction

### Plugin & Extension
- `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_SEED_DIR` (2.1.79) - Multiple seed directories (platform-delimited)
- `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_CACHE_DIR` (2.1.72) - Custom plugin cache
- `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_GIT_TIMEOUT_MS` (2.1.51) - Git timeout for plugins

### File System & Paths
- `XDG_DATA_HOME` (2.1.81) - Data directory on Linux
- `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA` (2.1.78) - Plugin persistent state directory

---

## Hook Events & Features

### Hook Events (v2.1.50+)
- `SessionStart` - Session initialization
- `SessionEnd` - Session closure (timeout configurable via `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSIONEND_HOOKS_TIMEOUT_MS`)
- `StopFailure` (2.1.78) - When turn ends due to API error
- `CwdChanged` (2.1.83) - Working directory changed
- `FileChanged` (2.1.83) - File changed
- `PostCompact` (2.1.76) - After compaction completes
- `InstructionsLoaded` (2.1.69) - When CLAUDE.md or `.claude/rules/*.md` loaded
- `ConfigChange` (2.1.49) - Configuration files changed
- `WorktreeCreate` (2.1.69) - Git worktree created
- `WorktreeRemove` (2.1.69) - Git worktree removed
- `ExitWorktree` (2.1.72) - Leave `EnterWorktree` session
- `TeammateIdle` (2.1.45, 2.1.47) - Multi-agent teammate idle with stop support
- `TaskCompleted` (2.1.45) - Task completed with stop support
- `Elicitation` (2.1.76) - MCP elicitation dialog intercept
- `ElicitationResult` (2.1.76) - MCP elicitation result
- `PreToolUse` (2.1.77) - Before tool execution
- `PostToolUse` - After tool execution
- `Stop` - Stop hook for agents

### Hook Features
- HTTP hooks (2.1.63) - POST JSON to URL and receive JSON response
- Hook source display in permission prompts (2.1.75) - Show settings/plugin/skill source
- Async hook completion messages (2.1.75) - Suppressed by default, visible with `--verbose`
- `last_assistant_message` field in Stop/SubagentStop hooks (2.1.47) - Final response text
- Progress messages support (2.1.49)
- Attachment messages support (2.1.75)
- Hook progress/attachment messages forking parentUuid chain (2.1.83)
- Plugin hooks firing on enable/disable (2.1.71)

### Hook Context & Data
- `transcript_path` (2.1.72) - Correct directory for resumed/forked sessions
- `worktree` field in status line hook commands (2.1.69) - name, path, branch, original repo
- `agent_id` (2.1.69) - For subagents
- `agent_type` (2.1.69) - For subagents and `--agent`
- `saved_hook_context` (2.1.29) - Session state resumption

## MCP & Tool Features

### MCP Tool Features
- Tool search with deferred loading (2.1.80) - Load tool schemas via `ToolSearch`
- MCP elicitation support (2.1.76) - Interactive dialog for structured input
- MCP OAuth with CIMD/SEP-991 (2.1.81) - Client ID Metadata Document support
- Fine-grained tool streaming (2.1.80) - Streaming through proxies, Bedrock, Vertex
- claude.ai MCP connectors (Slack, Gmail, etc.) (2.1.81, 2.1.46) - Available in interactive and `-p` mode
- MCP tool/resource deferred loading (2.1.80)
- Per-MCP-server elicitation hooks (2.1.76)

### MCP Configuration
- `/mcp` command (2.1.69) - Native MCP server management dialog
- MCP OAuth with manual URL paste fallback (2.1.63)
- OAuth metadata discovery caching (2.1.49)
- MCP server reconnect (2.1.50)
- Support for unauthenticated HTTP/SSE MCP servers (2.1.83)
- `--mcp-config` CLI flag with policy enforcement (2.1.83)

### WebFetch & Tool Improvements
- `WebFetch` identifies as `Claude-User` (2.1.83) - Recognize Claude Code in robots.txt
- Reduced `WebFetch` peak memory usage (2.1.83)
- `WebFetch` saves binary responses (PDFs, Office, audio) (2.1.69) - Decoded bytes with file extension
- Image processing failure handling (2.1.71) - Don't put oversized images in context

## CLI Flags & Commands

### New CLI Flags
- `--bare` (2.1.81) - Scripted `-p` calls: skip hooks, LSP, plugins, skill walks; requires API key
- `--channels` (2.1.80, 2.1.81) - Permission relay for MCP servers
- `--worktree` / `-w` (2.1.49) - Start in isolated git worktree
- `--from-pr` (2.1.27) - Resume session linked to GitHub PR
- `--name <name>` / `-n` (2.1.76) - Set session display name at startup
- `--console` (2.1.79) - Anthropic Console authentication
- `--model` (2.1.68) - Model selection
- `--effort` (2.1.72) - Effort level selection
- `--agent` (2.1.32) - Use specific agent

### New CLI Commands
- `claude auth login` (2.1.41) - Login
- `claude auth status` (2.1.41) - Check auth status
- `claude auth logout` (2.1.41) - Logout
- `claude auth login --console` (2.1.79) - Console authentication
- `claude remote-control` (2.1.51) - External environment serving
- `claude agents` (2.1.50) - List configured agents
- `claude doctor` (2.1.47) - Diagnostics
- `claude plugin validate` (2.1.77) - Validate plugins
- `claude mcp add` (2.1.30) - Add MCP server
- `claude mcp` (2.1.69) - MCP management

### New Slash Commands
- `/loop` (2.1.71) - Recurring interval commands (e.g., `/loop 5m check deploy`)
- `/simplify` (2.1.63) - Bundled command
- `/batch` (2.1.63) - Bundled command
- `/color` (2.1.75) - Set prompt-bar color (also `/color default`, `/color reset`)
- `/effort` (2.1.76) - Set model effort level (low/medium/high)
- `/copy` (2.1.59, 2.1.72) - Copy code blocks interactively; `/copy N` for Nth response
- `/memory` (2.1.59) - Manage auto-memory
- `/claude-api` (2.1.69) - Build with Claude API
- `/debug` (2.1.30) - Debug current session
- `/branch` (2.1.77) - Branch conversation (formerly `/fork`)
- `/fork` (2.1.77) - Alias for `/branch`
- `/rename` (2.1.75, 2.1.47) - Rename session with auto-generation
- `/reload-plugins` (2.1.69) - Activate pending plugin changes
- `/security-review` (2.1.70) - Security review
- `/status` (2.1.83) - Works while Claude is responding
- `/extra-usage` (2.1.50) - Enable extra usage (Max plan)

### Keybindings
- `Ctrl+X Ctrl+E` (2.1.83) - Open external editor (alias for `Ctrl+G`)
- `Ctrl+X Ctrl+K` (2.1.83) - Stop all background agents (was `Ctrl+F`)
- `Ctrl+L` (2.1.83) - Clear screen and force full redraw
- `Ctrl+U` (2.1.83) - Clear prompt input (or double-Esc)
- `Ctrl+O` (2.1.83) - Transcript mode with `/` search, `n`/`N` navigation
- `Shift+Down` (2.1.49) - Navigate teammates
- Transcript search (2.1.83) - Press `/` in transcript mode
- `chat:killAgents` keybinding (2.1.83) - Rebindable
- `chat:fastMode` keybinding (2.1.83) - Rebindable
- `chat:newline` keybinding (2.1.47) - Configurable multi-line input
- `voice:pushToTalk` keybinding (2.1.71) - Rebindable push-to-talk key

### Structured Outputs & API
- Structured outputs beta support (2.1.80) - Beta header management

- Compaction summary with images preserved (2.1.70)

  "new_string": "# Claude Code Configuration Optimization — Round 2\n\n## Context\n\nAfter the first optimization pass (saving ~19K tokens/session, CLAUDE.md 700→211 lines, 8 plugins disabled, stale data cleaned), the user wants to identify additional configuration improvements revealed by the Claude Code release notes (changelog up to v2.1.83).\n\nCross-referencing the full release notes against the current `~/.claude/settings.json` and project settings reveals 10 actionable optimizations across context management, agent efficiency, env var consolidation, and new features.\n\n## Current State (Key Config)\n\n- Model: Opus 4.6 with 1M context\n- 10 custom agents in `.claude/agents/` (only 2 specify `model:`, none specify `effort:`)\n- 15 MCP servers (8 global + 7 project)\n- `autoCompactPrompt` set but no `CLAUDE_AUTOCOMPACT_PCT_OVERRIDE` or `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW`\n- 4 individual DISABLE_ env vars instead of single `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC`\n- No `$schema` for JSON autocomplete\n- No `permissions.additionalDirectories` (vault is outside project root)\n- No `effortLevel` configured\n- No `PostCompact` hook for context preservation\n- No `worktree.symlinkDirectories`\n\n---\n\n## Plan\n\n### Task 1: Add context window tuning (HIGH IMPACT)\n\n**Problem:** With 1M context, compaction triggers at 95% = 950K tokens. This means the model works with increasingly degraded context for the last ~200K tokens before compaction fires. Earlier compaction preserves working memory quality.\n\n**Action:** Add to `~/.claude/settings.json` env:\n```json\n\"CLAUDE_AUTOCOMPACT_PCT_OVERRIDE\": \"85\",\n\"CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW\": \"800000\"\n```\n\nThis compacts at 85% of an 800K effective window = 680K tokens. You still have 1M available, but compaction fires ~270K tokens earlier, keeping the working context fresher. The `autoCompactPrompt` you already have controls WHAT is preserved during compaction.\n\n**Impact:** Better response quality in long sessions. No token cost change.\n\n**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`\n\n---\n\n### Task 2: Consolidate DISABLE_ env vars (CLEANUP)\n\n**Problem:** You have 4 individual disable flags:\n- `DISABLE_NON_ESSENTIAL_MODEL_CALLS: \"true\"`\n- `DISABLE_INSTALLATION_CHECKS: \"true\"`\n- `DISABLE_ERROR_REPORTING: \"true\"`\n- `DISABLE_TELEMETRY: \"true\"`\n\n`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC` (v2.1.50+) replaces all four with a single flag.\n\n**Action:** Replace the 4 flags with:\n```json\n\"CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC\": \"true\"\n```\n\nKeep `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER: \"false\"` separately (it's not covered by the umbrella flag).\n\n**Impact:** Cleaner config, same behavior. 3 fewer env vars.\n\n**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`\n\n---\n\n### Task 3: Add effort levels to custom agents (MEDIUM IMPACT)\n\n**Problem:** 10 custom agents in `.claude/agents/`. Only `kernel-researcher` and `kernel-writer` specify `model: sonnet`. None specify `effort:` frontmatter (available since v2.1.78). This means all agents use the session's default effort level, wasting tokens on read-only agents that don't need deep reasoning.\n\n**Action:** Add `effort:` frontmatter to each agent based on their role:\n\n| Agent | Current | Add | Rationale |\n|-------|---------|-----|-----------|\n| `code-reviewer` | no model/effort | `effort: medium` | Reviews need moderate reasoning |\n| `compound-executor` | no model/effort | `effort: high` | Complex compound cycles |\n| `compound-planner` | no model/effort | `effort: high` | Architectural planning |\n| `kernel-researcher` | `model: sonnet` | `effort: high` | Deep research |\n| `kernel-writer` | `model: sonnet` | `effort: high` | Complex kernel code |\n| `security-reviewer` | no model/effort | `effort: medium` | Pattern matching |\n| `simulation-runner` | no model/effort | `effort: medium` | Orchestration |\n| `skill-refiner` | no model/effort | `effort: medium` | Pattern extraction |\n| `skill-researcher` | no model/effort | `effort: medium` | Research |\n| `test-runner` | no model/effort | `effort: low` | Just runs pytest |\n| `tree-evolver` | no model/effort | `effort: high` | Optimization search |\n\n**Impact:** Token savings on low-effort agents, better quality on high-effort agents.\n\n**Files:** 10 files in `.claude/agents/`\n\n---\n\n### Task 4: Add JSON schema for autocomplete (LOW EFFORT, HIGH QOL)\n\n**Problem:** No `$schema` key in settings.json. Adding it enables autocomplete in VS Code / any JSON-aware editor when editing settings.\n\n**Action:** Add as first line of `~/.claude/settings.json`:\n```json\n\"$schema\": \"https://json.schemastore.org/claude-code-settings.json\"\n```\n\n**Impact:** Editor autocomplete for all 100+ settings keys.\n\n**Files:** `~/.claude/settings.json`, `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.claude/settings.json`\n\n---\n\n### Task 5: Add vault as additional directory (MEDIUM IMPACT)\n\n**Problem:** The vault lives at `~/vaults/cohezion-vault/` which is outside the project root (`~/dev/cohezion/`). Claude Code can only access files within the project root by default. When vault MCP tools work through the MCP server this is fine, but direct file reads/searches of vault content require `permissions.additionalDirectories`.\n\n**Action:** Add to project `.claude/settings.json`:\n```json\n\"permissions\": {\n    \"additionalDirectories\": [\"/home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault\"],\n    \"allow\": [...]\n}\n```\n\n**Impact:** Direct Read/Grep/Glob access to vault files without going through MCP.\n\n**File:** `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.claude/settings.json`\n\n---\n\n### Task 6: Add PostCompact hook for context preservation (MEDIUM IMPACT)\n\n**Problem:** When compaction fires, the `autoCompactPrompt` controls what's preserved, but there's no way to inject fresh context POST-compaction. The `PostCompact` hook (v2.1.76) runs after compaction completes — perfect for re-injecting critical session state.\n\n**Action:** Create `.claude/hooks/post-compact-context.sh`:\n```bash\n#!/bin/bash\n# PostCompact hook: re-inject critical context after compaction\n# Reads the current plan file and injects a summary\n\nPLAN_DIR=\"$HOME/.claude/plans\"\nLATEST_PLAN=$(ls -t \"$PLAN_DIR\"/*.md 2>/dev/null | head -1)\n\nif [ -n \"$LATEST_PLAN\" ]; then\n    echo \"[post-compact] Active plan: $(basename \"$LATEST_PLAN\")\"\nfi\n```\n\nRegister in global `~/.claude/settings.json` hooks:\n```json\n\"PostCompact\": [{\n    \"matcher\": \"all\",\n    \"hooks\": [{\n        \"type\": \"command\",\n        \"command\": \"/home/mike-anderson/.claude/hooks/post-compact-context.sh\"\n    }]\n}]\n```\n\n**Impact:** Critical plan context survives compaction.\n\n**Files:** `~/.claude/hooks/post-compact-context.sh` (new), `~/.claude/settings.json`\n\n---\n\n### Task 7: Add worktree symlink directories (LOW IMPACT)\n\n**Problem:** `worktree.sparsePaths` is set but `worktree.symlinkDirectories` isn't. When creating worktrees, heavy directories like `node_modules`, `.venv`, and `htmlcov` get fully copied instead of symlinked.\n\n**Action:** Add to `~/.claude/settings.json`:\n```json\n\"worktree\": {\n    \"sparsePaths\": [\"src/\", \"tests/\", \"config/\", \"scripts/\", \"research/\"],\n    \"symlinkDirectories\": [\"node_modules\", \".venv\", \"htmlcov\", \".cache\", \".mypy_cache\", \".ruff_cache\"]\n}\n```\n\n**Impact:** Faster worktree creation, less disk usage.\n\n**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`\n\n---\n\n### Task 8: Set global effort level (LOW EFFORT)\n\n**Problem:** No `effortLevel` configured. Opus 4.6 on Max/Team defaults to medium. For a compound engineering project with complex architectural decisions, `high` effort is generally more valuable.\n\n**Action:** Add to `~/.claude/settings.json`:\n```json\n\"effortLevel\": \"high\"\n```\n\nIndividual agents can override this via their `effort:` frontmatter (Task 3).\n\n**Impact:** Better reasoning quality by default. Higher token usage (~20-30% more per response).\n\n**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`\n\n---\n\n### Task 9: Set MCP timeouts (LOW IMPACT)\n\n**Problem:** No `MCP_TIMEOUT` or `MCP_TOOL_TIMEOUT` configured. With 15 MCP servers (7 stdio that spawn Python processes), startup can be slow. Default timeout may cause false failures.\n\n**Action:** Add to `~/.claude/settings.json` env:\n```json\n\"MCP_TIMEOUT\": \"30000\",\n\"MCP_TOOL_TIMEOUT\": \"60000\"\n```\n\n30s startup timeout (up from default ~10s), 60s execution timeout (some vault operations are slow).\n\n**Impact:** Fewer false MCP timeout failures.\n\n**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`\n\n---\n\n### Task 10: Set SessionEnd hooks timeout (LOW IMPACT)\n\n**Problem:** `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSIONEND_HOOKS_TIMEOUT_MS` defaults to 1500ms. Your project's SessionEnd hook calls `entire hooks claude-code session-end` which may need more time to complete cleanup.\n\n**Action:** Add to `~/.claude/settings.json` env:\n```json\n\"CLAUDE_CODE_SESSIONEND_HOOKS_TIMEOUT_MS\": \"5000\"\n```\n\n**Impact:** SessionEnd hooks complete reliably.\n\n**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n| Task | Category | Impact | Effort |\n|------|----------|--------|--------|\n| 1.
