## Approved Plan:
# Claude Code Configuration Optimization — Round 2

## Context

After the first optimization pass (saving ~19K tokens/session, CLAUDE.md 700→211 lines, 8 plugins disabled, stale data cleaned), the user wants to identify additional configuration improvements revealed by the Claude Code release notes (changelog up to v2.1.83).

Cross-referencing the full release notes against the current `~/.claude/settings.json` and project settings reveals 10 actionable optimizations across context management, agent efficiency, env var consolidation, and new features.

## Current State (Key Config)

- Model: Opus 4.6 with 1M context
- 10 custom agents in `.claude/agents/` (only 2 specify `model:`, none specify `effort:`)
- 15 MCP servers (8 global + 7 project)
- `autoCompactPrompt` set but no `CLAUDE_AUTOCOMPACT_PCT_OVERRIDE` or `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW`
- 4 individual DISABLE_ env vars instead of single `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC`
- No `$schema` for JSON autocomplete
- No `permissions.additionalDirectories` (vault is outside project root)
- No `effortLevel` configured
- No `PostCompact` hook for context preservation
- No `worktree.symlinkDirectories`

---

## Plan

### Task 1: Add context window tuning (HIGH IMPACT)

**Problem:** With 1M context, compaction triggers at 95% = 950K tokens. This means the model works with increasingly degraded context for the last ~200K tokens before compaction fires. Earlier compaction preserves working memory quality.

**Action:** Add to `~/.claude/settings.json` env:
```json
"CLAUDE_AUTOCOMPACT_PCT_OVERRIDE": "85",
"CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW": "800000"
```

This compacts at 85% of an 800K effective window = 680K tokens. You still have 1M available, but compaction fires ~270K tokens earlier, keeping the working context fresher. The `autoCompactPrompt` you already have controls WHAT is preserved during compaction.

**Impact:** Better response quality in long sessions. No token cost change.

**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`

---

### Task 2: Consolidate DISABLE_ env vars (CLEANUP)

**Problem:** You have 4 individual disable flags:
- `DISABLE_NON_ESSENTIAL_MODEL_CALLS: "true"`
- `DISABLE_INSTALLATION_CHECKS: "true"`
- `DISABLE_ERROR_REPORTING: "true"`
- `DISABLE_TELEMETRY: "true"`

`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC` (v2.1.50+) replaces all four with a single flag.

**Action:** Replace the 4 flags with:
```json
"CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC": "true"
```

Keep `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER: "false"` separately (it's not covered by the umbrella flag).

**Impact:** Cleaner config, same behavior. 3 fewer env vars.

**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`

---

### Task 3: Add effort levels to custom agents (MEDIUM IMPACT)

**Problem:** 10 custom agents in `.claude/agents/`. Only `kernel-researcher` and `kernel-writer` specify `model: sonnet`. None specify `effort:` frontmatter (available since v2.1.78). This means all agents use the session's default effort level, wasting tokens on read-only agents that don't need deep reasoning.

**Action:** Add `effort:` frontmatter to each agent based on their role:

| Agent | Current | Add | Rationale |
|-------|---------|-----|-----------|
| `code-reviewer` | no model/effort | `effort: medium` | Reviews need moderate reasoning |
| `compound-executor` | no model/effort | `effort: high` | Complex compound cycles |
| `compound-planner` | no model/effort | `effort: high` | Architectural planning |
| `kernel-researcher` | `model: sonnet` | `effort: high` | Deep research |
| `kernel-writer` | `model: sonnet` | `effort: high` | Complex kernel code |
| `security-reviewer` | no model/effort | `effort: medium` | Pattern matching |
| `simulation-runner` | no model/effort | `effort: medium` | Orchestration |
| `skill-refiner` | no model/effort | `effort: medium` | Pattern extraction |
| `skill-researcher` | no model/effort | `effort: medium` | Research |
| `test-runner` | no model/effort | `effort: low` | Just runs pytest |
| `tree-evolver` | no model/effort | `effort: high` | Optimization search |

**Impact:** Token savings on low-effort agents, better quality on high-effort agents.

**Files:** 10 files in `.claude/agents/`

---

### Task 4: Add JSON schema for autocomplete (LOW EFFORT, HIGH QOL)

**Problem:** No `$schema` key in settings.json. Adding it enables autocomplete in VS Code / any JSON-aware editor when editing settings.

**Action:** Add as first line of `~/.claude/settings.json`:
```json
"$schema": "https://json.schemastore.org/claude-code-settings.json"
```

**Impact:** Editor autocomplete for all 100+ settings keys.

**Files:** `~/.claude/settings.json`, `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.claude/settings.json`

---

### Task 5: Add vault as additional directory (MEDIUM IMPACT)

**Problem:** The vault lives at `~/vaults/cohezion-vault/` which is outside the project root (`~/dev/cohezion/`). Claude Code can only access files within the project root by default. When vault MCP tools work through the MCP server this is fine, but direct file reads/searches of vault content require `permissions.additionalDirectories`.

**Action:** Add to project `.claude/settings.json`:
```json
"permissions": {
    "additionalDirectories": ["/home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault"],
    "allow": [...]
}
```

**Impact:** Direct Read/Grep/Glob access to vault files without going through MCP.

**File:** `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.claude/settings.json`

---

### Task 6: Add PostCompact hook for context preservation (MEDIUM IMPACT)

**Problem:** When compaction fires, the `autoCompactPrompt` controls what's preserved, but there's no way to inject fresh context POST-compaction. The `PostCompact` hook (v2.1.76) runs after compaction completes — perfect for re-injecting critical session state.

**Action:** Create `.claude/hooks/post-compact-context.sh`:
```bash
#!/bin/bash
# PostCompact hook: re-inject critical context after compaction
# Reads the current plan file and injects a summary

PLAN_DIR="$HOME/.claude/plans"
LATEST_PLAN=$(ls -t "$PLAN_DIR"/*.md 2>/dev/null | head -1)

if [ -n "$LATEST_PLAN" ]; then
    echo "[post-compact] Active plan: $(basename "$LATEST_PLAN")"
fi
```

Register in global `~/.claude/settings.json` hooks:
```json
"PostCompact": [{
    "matcher": "all",
    "hooks": [{
        "type": "command",
        "command": "/home/mike-anderson/.claude/hooks/post-compact-context.sh"
    }]
}]
```

**Impact:** Critical plan context survives compaction.

**Files:** `~/.claude/hooks/post-compact-context.sh` (new), `~/.claude/settings.json`

---

### Task 7: Add worktree symlink directories (LOW IMPACT)

**Problem:** `worktree.sparsePaths` is set but `worktree.symlinkDirectories` isn't. When creating worktrees, heavy directories like `node_modules`, `.venv`, and `htmlcov` get fully copied instead of symlinked.

**Action:** Add to `~/.claude/settings.json`:
```json
"worktree": {
    "sparsePaths": ["src/", "tests/", "config/", "scripts/", "research/"],
    "symlinkDirectories": ["node_modules", ".venv", "htmlcov", ".cache", ".mypy_cache", ".ruff_cache"]
}
```

**Impact:** Faster worktree creation, less disk usage.

**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`

---

### Task 8: Set global effort level (LOW EFFORT)

**Problem:** No `effortLevel` configured. Opus 4.6 on Max/Team defaults to medium. For a compound engineering project with complex architectural decisions, `high` effort is generally more valuable.

**Action:** Add to `~/.claude/settings.json`:
```json
"effortLevel": "high"
```

Individual agents can override this via their `effort:` frontmatter (Task 3).

**Impact:** Better reasoning quality by default. Higher token usage (~20-30% more per response).

**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`

---

### Task 9: Set MCP timeouts (LOW IMPACT)

**Problem:** No `MCP_TIMEOUT` or `MCP_TOOL_TIMEOUT` configured. With 15 MCP servers (7 stdio that spawn Python processes), startup can be slow. Default timeout may cause false failures.

**Action:** Add to `~/.claude/settings.json` env:
```json
"MCP_TIMEOUT": "30000",
"MCP_TOOL_TIMEOUT": "60000"
```

30s startup timeout (up from default ~10s), 60s execution timeout (some vault operations are slow).

**Impact:** Fewer false MCP timeout failures.

**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`

---

### Task 10: Set SessionEnd hooks timeout (LOW IMPACT)

**Problem:** `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSIONEND_HOOKS_TIMEOUT_MS` defaults to 1500ms. Your project's SessionEnd hook calls `entire hooks claude-code session-end` which may need more time to complete cleanup.

**Action:** Add to `~/.claude/settings.json` env:
```json
"CLAUDE_CODE_SESSIONEND_HOOKS_TIMEOUT_MS": "5000"
```

**Impact:** SessionEnd hooks complete reliably.

**File:** `~/.claude/settings.json`

---

## Summary

| Task | Category | Impact | Effort |
|------|----------|--------|--------|
| 1. Context window tuning | Performance | High | 2 env vars |
| 2. Consolidate DISABLE_ flags | Cleanup | Low | Replace 4 with 1 |

| 10. SessionEnd timeout | Reliability | Low | 1 env var |

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/.claude/hooks/post-compact-context.sh",
  "content": "#!/bin/bash\n# PostCompact hook: re-inject critical context after compaction\n# Prints the active plan file name so it survives compaction.\n\nPLAN_DIR=\"$HOME/.claude/plans\"\nLATEST_PLAN=$(ls -t \"$PLAN_DIR\"/*.md 2>/dev/null | head -1)\n\nif [ -n \"$LATEST_PLAN\" ]; then\n    echo \"[post-compact] Active plan: $(basename \"$LATEST_PLAN\")\"\nfi\n"
}
```

Verifying:\"\nfor f in *.md; do\n  effort=$(grep \"^effort:\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"MISSING\")\n  echo \"  $f: $effort\"\ndone",

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**All 10 optimizations applied.** Here's the summary of what changed:

| # | Change | Where |
|---|--------|-------|
| 1 | Context compacts at 85% of 800K (680K) instead of 95% of 1M (950K) | env vars |
| 2 | 4 DISABLE_ flags → 1 `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC` | env vars |
| 3 | 11 agents now have `effort:` frontmatter (low/medium/high) | .claude/agents/ |
| 4 | `$schema` added for JSON autocomplete | settings.json |
| 5 | Vault directory accessible directly via `additionalDirectories` | project settings |
| 6 | PostCompact hook re-injects active plan name | new hook |
| 7 | Worktree symlinks for node_modules, .venv, .cache, etc. | worktree config |
| 8 | Global effort level set to `high` | settings.json |
| 9 | MCP timeouts: 30s startup, 60s execution | env vars |
| 10 | SessionEnd hooks timeout: 5s (up from 1.5s) | env vars |

**Why the context window tuning matters most:** At 1M context, the last ~300K tokens before compaction are increasingly stale — the model's attention gets diluted across too much noise. By treating 800K as the effective window and compacting at 85%, you get compaction at 680K instead of 950K. That's 270K fewer tokens of degraded context per session. Your `autoCompactPrompt` already specifies what to preserve (plans, tasks, test results, decisions) — now it fires 40% earlier.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

All 10 config optimizations applied and both settings files validated. Changes take effect in your next session. Want me to commit these?

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Lesson learned: `sed` edits get reverted by external file watchers.** The `entire` hooks system appears to snapshot and restore `.claude/` files on certain events. The Edit tool's changes persisted because Claude Code tracks them internally, while raw `sed` operates below that layer. This is actually a good safety mechanism — it prevents accidental file corruption from scripts — but it means agent file edits must go through Claude Code's tool system.

Task 3: Recreate MCP health hook\n\n**File:** `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.claude/hooks/mcp-health-check.sh`\n\nThe file was created by a team agent but didn't persist (likely created in a worktree or temp context). Recreate it:\n\n```bash\n#!/bin/bash\n# MCP Health Check — SessionStart hook\n# Pings HTTP-accessible MCP servers. Non-blocking (always exits 0).\n\nDEGRADED=()\n\nif ! curl -sf --max-time 3 \"http://localhost:8360/health\" > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n    DEGRADED+=(\"cohezion-vault (http://localhost:8360)\")\nfi\n\nif ! curl -sf --max-time 2 \"http://localhost:8000/health\" > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n    DEGRADED+=(\"surrealdb (http://localhost:8000)\")\nfi\n\nif ! curl -sf --max-time 2 \"http://localhost:11434/api/tags\" > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n    DEGRADED+=(\"ollama (http://localhost:11434)\")\nfi\n\nif [ ${#DEGRADED[@]} -gt 0 ]; then\n    echo \"[mcp-health-check] Warning: ${#DEGRADED[@]} service(s) unreachable:\"\n    for svc in \"${DEGRADED[@]}\"; do\n        echo \"  - $svc\"\n    done\n    echo \"[mcp-health-check] Run vault_health_check for full diagnostics, or /wake to start services.\"\nfi\n```\n\nMake executable: `chmod +x .claude/hooks/mcp-health-check.sh`\n\n### Task 4: Add MCP tools for skill health metrics\n\n**File:** `src/cohezion/mcp/compound_server.py`\n\nAdd 3 tools to the existing compound MCP server:\n\n```python\n@mcp.tool()\nasync def skill_health_get(skill_name: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Get health metrics for a specific skill.\"\"\"\n    tracker = SkillHealthTracker()\n    record = tracker.get_health(skill_name)\n    if not record:\n        return {\"error\": f\"No health data for skill: {skill_name}\"}\n    return asdict(record)\n\n@mcp.tool()\nasync def skill_health_stale(days: int = 30) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"List skills not used in N days.\"\"\"\n    tracker = SkillHealthTracker()\n    return {\"stale_skills\": tracker.get_stale_skills(days)}\n\n@mcp.tool()\nasync def skill_health_dashboard() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Get health metrics for all tracked skills, sorted by health score.\"\"\"\n    tracker = SkillHealthTracker()\n    records = tracker.get_all_health()\n    return {\"skills\": [asdict(r) for r in records], \"count\": len(records)}\n```\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `python3 -c \"from cohezion.compound import SkillHealthTracker\"` — import works\n2. `python3 -c \"import json; d=json.load(open('src/cohezion/skills/skill_registry.json')); print(len(d))\"` — count increased by 7\n3. `bash .claude/hooks/mcp-health-check.sh` — exits 0, prints warnings if services down\n4. `uv run pytest tests/compound/ -q` — all tests pass\n",
